# Seed 59 on the AMD Ryzen 7 PRO 8840HS - seed 58's start with the tag-2 requirement: small at both widths, the requirement's cost replicated; and a fifth of its front dies on the corpus at 32 bits (Iteration 398)

`seed 59 tag2 12h widths nocarry then experiment compare-fronts.py --rounds 6 --widths 8,4 --frontier --cpus 2,8` at
31b591c59 (Iteration 384): seed 58's start (nocarry, the same evolved founders), the tag-2 requirement the designed
difference. The first chain on Iteration 379's remote path: the checks started 15:35:59, the evolution 15:42:05 - six
minutes, where seed 58 waited 55; it evolved 8 h 55 min (seed 58: 8 h 01). 35,862 designs, generations 0-143: 27,185
alive, 8,489 screened (24.5%), 188 refused at the reach limit; no compile death (require() holds bytehdr on).

**The verdict - small at both widths.** At 64 bits from both CPUs (the owner's rule of 389; the session printed cpu
2's): fastest +1.2%, smallest -11.12%, memory -4 KB, hypervolume +0.4%, best Q -4.1% - small, and small on each CPU
alone (cpu 2 +0.8% / best Q -3.8%; cpu 8 +2.4% / -0.8%). At 32 bits its fastest 0.344 (cd9d470f51), 9.2% behind the
others' best (seed 58's plain designs). The Q-best of all runs stays seed 57's e6b5f1749e (Q 0.206 of s6's).

**The constraint finding replicated.** Against seed 58 - one start, the requirement the difference: the small end
15,915 bytes against 14,146 (+12.5%), the 32-bit fastest 0.344 against 0.310 (+11%), the 64-bit fast end alike (inside
the tie). The requirement costs the small end and 32 bits, and only them.

**A fault: 34 of the front's 171 die on the corpus at 32 bits** (why32 "died: corpus"; all jit=1, scale=0, tos=0); in
the database 2,057 such, and 326 that did not compile at 32 bits. Their 64-bit engines are sound; selection counts a
missing 32-bit speed as the worst (evolve.py, Iteration 336), so they hold the front by their 64-bit objectives.
Seed 58's front had none. HANDOFF open item 0f.

**From inside** (review-checks.py progress): diversity holds, 0.516 at the end (seed 58's tag-2 family: 0.47); but
the gains stopped early - the smallest last at generation 88 of 143, none in the last third; speed's last 2% step at
81. Seed 58, free, gained to generation 130: within the requirement the search converged early.

**Held out** (heldout.py): sieve 0.909 over the front, 0.753 its fastest third, 0.123 its fastest tenth; the regret
+8.7% (5f63a84a70 against df74ca36c3; seeds 57 and 58: 3.0%, 4.5%).

**Predictions (results/predictions.md): 3 met, 1 met but for a clause, 1 missed.** 1 met (small); 2 met (15,915: 1.8%
above the carried 15,633, 12.5% above seed 58's 14,146); 3 missed (32 bits 11% behind seed 58, not 15%); 4 met (no
L_t2call4 death); 5 met - the run six minutes after the checks, the probes in every report - but for its last clause:
the Tegra's copy was 7,219 KB/s, not a few KB/s.

**The checks** (results/xcheck-seed59.md), of seed 58's front: dream 64-bit Spearman 0.959 and 0.963, at 32 bits
0.651, the Tegra 0.709 - each against the laptop's 64-bit speeds, which rank a two-encoding front poorly at 32 bits
(seed 58's own speed against its 32-bit speed: 0.573; open item 0g). No rejected design faster than the front's
fastest on any host. The probes: 8.2-8.6 MB copied in 0-1 s (7,219 KB/s to the Tegra, 68,724-87,740 to dream); the
Tegra's long wait is its own start, 36 minutes (its build), not the copy - Iteration 380's reading corrected; the
hosts' load 1.1-1.4.

---
# Evolved VM designs

27373 designs evaluated, 27185 alive, 8489 screened - not measured (Iteration 200). Speed is the geometric mean of median cpu time / hand-made s6, long workloads (Iteration 100) over kernel, fib, corpus, loop, bubble, ackermann,
relative to s6-cv8b; size is the self-hosting image, binary the stripped engine. sieve, parse is held out.
The front is over three objectives, equal (Iteration 75): speed, binary + image, peak memory - memory within 8 KB a tie (Iteration 77).


The Q-best design (binary + image x run time x memory - the knee; information): c5a2d4e06a, speed 0.130, binary + image 30,325, memory 168 KB - Q 0.203 of s6-cv8b's.

## The front's genomes

Each design above, and any other the front was measured again with, as one JSON line: its record without the raw timings. A tool that takes a database (--db) reads this report as one - the lines that are not JSON are skipped - so results/ is all a new chat needs to rebuild a front design.
